# Starlight Linear and Relative Layout Algorithm Coverage

Sources:
- `core/renderer/starlight/docs/linear-layout.md`
- `core/renderer/starlight/docs/relative-layout.md`
- `core/renderer/starlight/layout/linear_layout_algorithm.cc`
- `core/renderer/starlight/layout/relative_layout_algorithm.cc`

This inventory is intentionally conservative. Linear and relative layout are
Starlight module specifications, not W3C Recommendations. Passing C++ parity
tests proves compatibility for represented scenarios, but it does not prove
that every style-resolved tree covered by the Starlight specs has been audited.

## Current Surface Boundaries

- The layout engine consumes style resolver output. Style values are assumed to
  be syntactically valid before they reach linear or relative layout.
- Invalid CSS syntax is not a layout repair case.
- If a syntactically valid style value is ignored, mapped, falls back, or still
  participates in linear/relative layout, that is layout-engine logic.
- `display: none` direct children are laid out as hidden zero-sized subtrees
  and do not become linear or relative items.
- `display: none` absolute and fixed nodes are hidden zero-sized subtrees and
  skip out-of-flow measurement/alignment. Fixed hidden descendants are still
  visited by the root fixed-node flow and export at the root border-bound
  origin, matching C++ `InitializeFixedNode` plus `HideLayoutObject`.
- Fixed descendants under a `display: none` ancestor still enter the root
  fixed-node set. C++ marks them hidden through ancestor `HideLayoutObject`,
  skips fixed measurement via `GetShouldDisplayNone`, but still runs fixed
  alignment against the root containing block while preserving zero size.
- `position: relative` items remain in normal flow. C++ applies left/right and
  top/bottom offsets after in-flow and out-of-flow alignment in
  `HandleRelativePosition`; start-side offsets win over end-side offsets, and
  relative-layout dependencies use the pre-offset position.
- `position: sticky` items also remain in normal flow. C++ exports sticky
  constraints after normal item alignment through `UpdateStickyItemPosition`;
  sticky constraints do not move sibling/dependency placement.
- `physical_pixels_per_layout_unit` is an export-stage behavior. C++
  `LayoutObject::RoundToPixelGrid` rounds offset, size, padding, border, and
  sticky constraints from absolute coordinates after the layout algorithm has
  produced raw positions.
- `visibility: hidden` and non-flex/table `visibility: collapse` children
  remain linear or relative items unless they also have `display: none`.
- The standalone C++ public C API still rejects non-visible `visibility`
  values, so visibility participation is currently covered by Rust unit tests
  and native C++ head-to-head tests, not standalone source-built tests.

## Current Coverage Evidence

- 2026-07-08 targeted command:
  `cargo llvm-cov -q -p starlight_layout -p starlight_parity --test linear_layout_tests --test relative_display_layout_tests --test linear_relative_algorithm_coverage_tests --summary-only --show-missing-lines`
- Result for `crates/starlight_layout/src/engine/relative.rs`: 100.00%
  regions, functions, and lines.
- Result for `crates/starlight_layout/src/engine/linear.rs`: 99.86%
  regions, 97.83% functions, and 99.90% lines. LCOV had no `DA:*,0`
  source line for `linear.rs`; the remaining summary miss is from
  generic/instantiation coverage accounting, not an uncovered source line.
- 2026-07-09 source-built standalone command:
  `STARLIGHT_CPP_NATIVE_STANDALONE_BUILD_FROM_SOURCE=1 cargo test -q --manifest-path core/renderer/starlight/rust/Cargo.toml -p starlight_parity --features native-standalone --test standalone_head_to_head_tests`
- Result: 948 passed, 8 ignored.

ordered duplicate-id lookup with display-none anchors skipped across right_of, left_of, bottom_of, top_of, align-left, align-right, align-top, align-bottom, layout-once combined-axis, and wrap-content cases, parent and sibling same-side alignment precedence over sibling after/before constraints, parent as after/before adjacency reference on all four physical sides, all sibling edge reference properties, standalone/public-C-API sibling-edge coverage, scope-specific horizontal and vertical dependency resolution, layout-once combined-axis dependency resolution, layout-once primary-align conflict precedence, layout-once missing-primary fallback reference resolution, measured mixed parent/sibling and sibling/sibling stretch constraints across both axes, measured parent-adjacency to sibling-align-end stretch references, measured sibling-align parent-edge, sibling-align sibling-before/after, sibling-after sibling-align-end, sibling-align-start sibling-align-end, and adjacency right_of/left_of/bottom_of/top_of stretch references including percent/calc margin and root padding/border content-origin variants, layout-once parent after/before adjacency references on all four physical sides plus measured parent-adjacency to sibling-align-end stretch references, layout-once measured mixed stretch reference resolution including sibling-align parent-edge, sibling-align sibling-before/after, sibling-after sibling-align-end, sibling-align-start sibling-align-end, adjacency right_of/left_of/bottom_of/top_of percent/calc margin root padding/border variants, and wrap-content sibling/sibling references, two-pass wrap-content measured mixed reference resolution including sibling-align parent-edge, sibling-align sibling-before/after, sibling-after sibling-align-end, sibling-align-start sibling-align-end, and adjacency right_of/left_of/bottom_of/top_of percent/calc margin root padding/border references, two-pass ordered duplicate-id/display-none sibling/sibling reference resolution for adjacency and sibling-align edges across both axes, and generated constraint-mode/layout-once dependency resolution have C++ parity coverage. margin-bound positioning, static-start padding/border content-origin positioning with fixed, percent, and calc margins, RTL static-start fallback through the C++ `HorizontalFront` direction path for absolute/fixed children with fixed, percent, and calc margins, absolute start/end percent inset and size resolution, absolute start/end inset placement with percent margins, absolute calc start/end inset placement with percent margins, absolute calc end-inset placement with percent margins plus padding/border containing-block origin, absolute auto-size stretch between start/end insets with fixed, percent, or calc inputs, absolute single-inset AtMost constraint stripping, relative root padding-box containing-block sizing for absolute children, absolute display-none skip of out-of-flow measurement/alignment under wrap-content and definite containers, absolute descendants under display-none ancestors staying hidden rather than entering the relative out-of-flow pass, explicit absolute start/end inset override over static start with and without margins, explicit absolute end-inset margin-bound positioning including bottom-only percent-margin placement, explicit absolute paired-inset start priority with definite size with fixed, percent, or calc insets and with/without margins, horizontal and vertical absolute percent paired-inset start priority with percent margins, fixed start/end percent inset and size resolution, fixed start/end inset placement with percent margins resolved against the root containing block, fixed calc start/end inset placement with percent margins resolved against the root containing block, fixed calc right-only placement with calc horizontal margins and root padding/border origin, fixed calc end-inset placement with percent margins plus root padding/border containing-block origin, fixed auto-size stretch between start/end insets with fixed, percent, or calc inputs, fixed single-inset AtMost constraint stripping, relative root padding-box offsets for fixed descendants, fixed display-none descendant hiding at the root border-bound origin without root containing-block measurement, fixed descendants under display-none ancestors keeping zero size while still aligning against the root fixed containing block, fixed static-start root padding/border content-origin positioning with fixed, percent, and calc margins, explicit fixed start/end inset override over static start with and without margins, explicit fixed end-inset margin-bound positioning, explicit fixed paired-inset start priority with definite size with fixed, percent, or calc insets and with/without margins, horizontal and vertical fixed percent paired-inset start priority with percent margins, physical-pixel rounded absolute inset placement, absolute auto-size between insets, fixed auto-size between insets, nested fixed export against the root containing block, and root containing-block behavior. Completion still needs a final C++ branch audit for remaining out-of-flow inset/static-position combinations where expressible; there is no known parity gap in the covered paths.
